Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the approximation object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 615. The moduli space has dimension at most 589 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_615(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 615. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^589(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 215.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The approximation variety V defined by optimality condition constraints admits a stratification into 83 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 226 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 83 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the approximation structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 226. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 215.4 (Invariance Principle)
